# fm CLI coverage: what each read op does not report

Checked against fm 0.6.0 (29816214) on 2026-09-14. Reference: the Save as XML export of the Ooe solution.

Every probe below ran in one invocation:

```
fm --file=fmnet://localhost/ooe --username=admin --keychain --no-prompt --abort-on-error=false --out=/var/folders/1h/xw_j9f891q1_988625vlsw5c0000gp/T/fm-adt-LyHdSu/batch.out.ndjson /var/folders/1h/xw_j9f891q1_988625vlsw5c0000gp/T/fm-adt-LyHdSu/batch.ops.ndjson
```

302 kinds, 1626 attributes not reported.

11 kinds not yet verified.

## Summary

| Read op | Kinds | Attributes not reported | The story |
|---|---|---|---|
| evaluate:calculation | 1 | 0 | — |
| No read op exists | 4 (1 unverified) | 138 | File Options, Themes, Calculation tokens |
| read:account | 8 | 33 | — |
| read:authorization | 1 | 4 | — |
| read:baseDirectory | 1 | 4 | — |
| read:customFunction | 1 | 4 | — |
| read:customMenu | 2 | 12 | — |
| read:customMenuSet | 1 | 3 | — |
| read:extendedPrivilege | 1 | 4 | — |
| read:externalDataSource | 1 | 13 | — |
| read:field | 14 | 45 | — |
| read:layout | 38 (10 unverified) | 431 | Layout parts, Sort specifications, Conditional formatting |

### The biggest gaps

- **Layout parts** — `read:layout` reports no `contents.parts` key at all, so nothing about a part is readable: not the part list, not a part’s type, name, height, page-break options or a sub-summary’s break field. Ten part kinds are described here and none could be checked against fm, because the container they would live in is absent. (95 attributes across 10 kinds.)
- **File Options** — There is no op that reads a file’s own options: `read:file` is refused with `unknown_catalog`. The opening script, the account to log in with, the minimum FileMaker version, the window and menubar hide settings and the rest of the Metadata block are unreachable. (35 attributes across 1 kind.)
- **Themes** — fm names the theme a layout wears and reports nothing inside it. Every style the theme defines — the per-state fills, strokes, fonts and paddings that decide what a layout actually looks like — has no op to read it. (136 attributes across 1 kind.)
- **Sort specifications** — Wherever FileMaker stores a sort order — a portal’s, a value list’s, a relationship’s — fm reports that sorting is on but not the fields sorted by or their directions. (23 attributes across 4 kinds.)
- **Modification info** — Almost every catalog element in the export carries who changed it, when, and how many times. fm reports none of the triple, on any kind and under any op — the single most widely repeated gap in this register. (117 attributes across 39 kinds.)
- **Conditional formatting** — A layout object’s conditional formatting rules — the condition, the comparison value and the formatting each rule applies — are not reported, so a layout cannot be reproduced from what fm says about it. (32 attributes across 3 kinds.)
- **Calculation tokens** — fm hands back a formula as text and never as its parts. `validate:calculation` says valid or invalid and names no field, occurrence, variable, custom function, script or plug-in function the formula refers to, so "where is this field used" cannot be answered. (2 attributes across 2 kinds.)

## Packed words fm reports raw

100 kinds carry a packed options word that fm emits verbatim and decodes not at all: the raw number is present in the response, and none of the bits inside it is named.
The ask is the decode, not the number. FileMaker knows what each bit of each word means — the same table the Script Workspace uses to print a step’s options — and the register can only record which bits it has attributed by measurement.

## evaluate:calculation

### fileFact:encryption

Register id: `file-encryption`. Probe: `{"op":"evaluate:calculation","calculation":"Get ( EncryptionState )"}`.

Every known attribute is reported.

Reported (1): `value`.

Last checked fm 0.6.0 (29816214) on 2026-09-14; probe 32 of 74 in one invocation.

Response, the instance this entry describes (exit 1), selected by `(root)`:

```json
{
 "kind": "calculation",
 "value": "0",
 "dataType": "text"
}
```

Full response: `gaps/evidence/0.6.0-29816214/462c612c.ndjson` (stdout and stderr verbatim, one file per probe).

## No read op exists

### calculation

Register id: `calculation-tokens`. Probe: `{"op":"validate:calculation","calculation":"Contacts::Name & $$SMTP Server","context":"Contacts"}`.

**Not reported**

| Attribute | Known from | SaXML path |
|---|---|---|
| tokenised references of a formula (fields, occurrences, variables, functions, scripts) | SaXML DDR_INFO writes one Chunk per reference a formula makes — FieldReference, VariableReference, FunctionRef, CustomFunctionRef and ScriptRef are the FileMaker 2026 chunk types — which is how a DDR answers "where is this field used". fm reports calculation text only; `validate:calculation` reports valid/invalid and names no token of the formula it just parsed. | `DDR_INFO/Chunk[type=FieldReference|VariableReference|FunctionRef|CustomFunctionRef|ScriptRef]` |

Last checked fm 0.6.0 (29816214) on 2026-09-14; probe 73 of 74 in one invocation.

Response, the instance this entry describes (exit 1), selected by `(root)`:

```json
{
 "kind": "calculation",
 "for": "standalone",
 "valid": true
}
```

Full response: `gaps/evidence/0.6.0-29816214/7fca32c8.ndjson` (stdout and stderr verbatim, one file per probe).

### calculation:plugin

Register id: `plugin-call-sites`. Probe: `{"op":"validate:calculation","calculation":"BE_Version"}`.

**Not reported**

| Attribute | Known from | SaXML path |
|---|---|---|
| plug-in function call site in a formula | SaXML DDR_INFO writes a FunctionRef chunk for a call to a plug-in function; fm help: plug-in functions are the one gap of the evaluator, which answers calc_unknown_function for a function no installed plug-in declares, so a formula’s plug-in call sites cannot be recovered from fm at all. | `DDR_INFO/Chunk[type=FunctionRef] (plug-in function)` |

Last checked fm 0.6.0 (29816214) on 2026-09-14; probe 74 of 74 in one invocation.

Response, the instance this entry describes (exit 1), selected by `(root)`:

```json
{
 "kind": "calculation",
 "for": "standalone",
 "valid": false,
 "error": {
  "code": "table_missing",
  "engineCode": 106,
  "message": "the calculation references a table occurrence that does not exist, or a field on one not related to the evaluation context",
  "errorBegin": 0,
  "errorEnd": 10
 },
 "warnings": [
  "this formula is NOT valid. The line reports status \"ok\" and exits 0 because the question -- \"is this valid?\" -- was answered; the ANSWER is \"valid\": false. Branch on result.valid, never on status alone. status \"error\" here would mean the question could not be answered at all (an unknown 'context' occurrence)."
 ]
}
```

Full response: `gaps/evidence/0.6.0-29816214/80c26ca5.ndjson` (stdout and stderr verbatim, one file per probe).

| account has a password | SaXML AccountsCatalog Authentication/PasswordEncrypted (element present); File > Manage > Security > Accounts (the New/Edit Account dialog) > Password. The register records the same as a CONFIRMED CLI GAP on step:87 | `action/Step/ParameterValues/Parameter/Parameter/FieldReference/repetition/Calculation/Calculation/Text` |

| auto-enter calculated value is on | SaXML FieldCatalog AutoEnter/Calculated@enable; Field Options > Auto-Enter > Calculated value checkbox. fm emits options.autoEnter.calculation only when options.autoEnter.type is "calc". The probe field carries AutoEnter@type="ConstantData" with a retained Calculated@enable="True" and its formula, and fm 0.6.0 reports autoEnter {type "constant", constant "456"} with no calculation object at all (gaps/evidence/0.6.0-29816214/e9a6cd8b.ndjson); fmai records the same loss. So a formula FileMaker keeps behind a switched-off mode, and the switch itself, are not reported. | `AutoEnter/Calculated@enable` |

### object:Container

Register id: `layout-object:container`. Probe: `{"op":"read:layout","name":"My Layout for TestTable","detail":true}` selecting `**objects[id=23]`.

**Not reported**

| Attribute | Known from | SaXML path |
|---|---|---|
| object type | SaXML LayoutCatalog @type; Layout mode > Insert > Field on a container field. fm collapses Container into the plain field control: it reports type "field" and control "editBox", exactly what a text Edit Box reports, so a Container object cannot be told from an Edit Box in fm 0.6.0 (verified: Ooe object 23 is type="Container" in the export and reads back control "editBox") | `@type` |

| local style overrides (the object’s own CSS) | SaXML LayoutCatalog LocalCSS; Inspector > Appearance, any property changed away from the style (the style name then shows a change marker). fm decodes exactly one property out of this body — text-align, reported as textAlign (verified on Text object 81) — and reports none of the colours, borders, fonts, sizes or underline | `LocalCSS` |
| style kind (named theme style vs local override) | SaXML LayoutCatalog LocalCSS@type; Inspector > Appearance: ThemeCustomStyle means the object wears a named style, ObjectCustomStyle means it carries an unnamed local override. fm reports style only in the first case, so the second case is invisible | `LocalCSS@type` |
